| ae-corporate-tax / base / 3 | frontmatter: description: "Use this skill whenever asked about UAE Corporate Tax for freelancers, sole establishments, or small businesses. Trigger on phrases like \"how much tax do I pay in UAE\", \"corporate tax UAE\", \"CT return\", \"FTA\", \"small business relief\", \"free zone tax\", \"qualifying free zone person\", \"AED 375,000\", \"9% tax\", \"taxable income UAE\", \"corporate tax registration\", \"UAE tax return\", \"self-employed tax UAE\", \"freelancer tax Dubai\", \"EmaraTax\", or any question about computing or filing UAE corporate tax. This skill covers the 0%/9% rate structure, small business relief (revenue under AED 3M), qualifying free zone person rules, deductible and non-deductible expenses, transfer pricing, registration requirements, and filing deadlines. Note: the UAE has NO personal income tax — self-employed individuals and sole establishments are subject to corporate tax. ALWAYS read this skill before touching any UAE corporate tax work." | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 3 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | ae-corporate-tax / base / 17 | UAE Corporate Tax: 2026 rates, scope, Small Business Relief, free zones and filing: Tax year 2026. In the UAE the tax period is the business's financial year, so "2026" here means tax periods running in 2026 (for most businesses, the calendar year 1 January to 31 December 2026). The rules below are the law as published on tax.gov.ae and mof.gov.ae on 25 September 2026. A short dated section covers returns for periods ending in 2025, which are being filed now. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 18 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. These are taxable from the first dirham of taxable income. There is no turnover threshold for a juridical person (Corporate Tax Law, Article 11(3)). | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 24 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | ae-corporate-tax / base / 24 | Scope and who this is for: - Individuals (natural persons) running a business in the UAE: sole establishments, freelancers and other self-employed people. They are taxable only when their total business turnover is more than AED 1,000,000 in a calendar year (Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023, Article 2). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 25 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-corporate-tax / base / 32 | Ask the client first: ## Ask the client first | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 33 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 34 | Ask the client first: - **What kind of person is the business?** A company (LLC, free zone company, branch), a sole establishment or freelancer (natural person), or a partnership? A juridical person is in scope from the first dirham; a natural person only above AED 1,000,000 of business turnover in a calendar year. ([Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Legislation/05-06-2023/3/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2049%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 35 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 35 | Ask the client first: - **Financial year end and first tax period.** This sets the return and payment deadline (9 months after the period ends). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 36 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 36 | Ask the client first: - **Revenue for this period and for every earlier tax period.** Small Business Relief fails permanently once any period's revenue has been over AED 3,000,000. ([Ministerial Decision No. 73 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Corporate%20Tax/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%2073%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 36 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 37 | Ask the client first: - **Is the business part of a multinational group?** Ask for group consolidated revenue. A constituent company of a multinational group, as defined in Cabinet Decision No. 44 of 2020 (groups with consolidated revenue of at least AED 3.15 billion), cannot use Small Business Relief ([Ministerial Decision No. 73 of 2023, Article 3](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Corporate%20Tax/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%2073%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf); [Small Business Relief Guide](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Guides/CT/Small%20Business%20Relief%20Guide%20-%20EN%20-%2029%2008%202023.pdf)). Groups with EUR 750 million or more may be in scope of top-up tax. ([Cabinet Decision No. 142 of 2024](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Cabinet-Decision-No-142-of-2024-on-Top-up-Tax-on-MNEs.pdf)) | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; Article 20 base retained | Accounting/cash-basis eligibility and separate SBR MNE definition held; distinct master/local-file threshold retained. |

| ae-corporate-tax / base / 46 | The method, step by step: ## The method, step by step | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 42 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 48 | The method, step by step: 1. **Decide whether the person is a Taxable Person.** A juridical person resident in the UAE is always one, including a free zone company. A natural person is one only if their business turnover is more than AED 1,000,000 in the calendar year, not counting wages, personal investment income or real estate investment income. Check exemptions (government entities, qualifying public benefit entities, qualifying funds and others under Article 4) and refer them out. ([Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Legislation/05-06-2023/3/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2049%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 44 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 49 | The method, step by step: 2. **Check registration.** Every Taxable Person registers on EmaraTax by the deadline in FTA Decision No. 3 of 2024 (table below), including businesses that will claim Small Business Relief and free zone companies. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 45 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 50 | The method, step by step: 3. **Fix the tax period.** It is the financial year, meaning the Gregorian calendar year or the 12-month period for which financial statements are prepared (Article 57). Changing it needs an application to the FTA (Article 58). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 46 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 51 | The method, step by step: 4. **Test Small Business Relief** for a resident person. Revenue must be AED 3,000,000 or less in this period *and in every previous period*. The period must end on or before 31 December 2029. The person must not be a QFZP or a constituent company of a multinational group. If eligible and the client wants it, elect it on the return. Taxable income is then treated as nil and a simplified return is filed. ([Ministerial Decision No. 73 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Corporate%20Tax/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%2073%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 47 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 52 | The method, step by step: 5. **For a free zone company, test QFZP status**: adequate substance, qualifying income, the de minimis test, arm's length compliance with transfer pricing documentation, audited financial statements, and no election out. If any condition fails, it is taxed like a mainland company for that period and the next four. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 48 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 53 | The method, step by step: 6. **Compute taxable income** (Article 20): start from accounting net profit under IFRS (or IFRS for SMEs where allowed). Remove exempt income (for example qualifying dividends and participation income, Articles 22-23). Add back non-deductible items: 50% of entertainment, fines, bribes, owner drawings, Corporate Tax itself, recoverable input VAT, and donations to bodies that are not qualifying public benefit entities (Articles 28, 32, 33). Apportion mixed personal and business costs. ([Corporate Tax Law](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Federal-Decree-Law-No.-47-of-2022-and-its-amendments-en-v13.1.26.pdf)) | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; Article 20 base retained | Accounting/cash-basis eligibility and separate SBR MNE definition held; distinct master/local-file threshold retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 54 | The method, step by step: 7. **Apply the interest limitation** if net interest expense is more than AED 12,000,000: the deduction is the higher of AED 12,000,000 and 30% of tax-adjusted EBITDA. The excess carries forward for 10 periods. ([Ministerial Decision No. 126 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2023/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%20126%20of%202023%20on%20the%20General%20Interest%20Deduction%20Limitation%20Rule%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 50 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 55 | The method, step by step: 8. **Adjust related party and connected person transactions to arm's length** (Articles 34-36). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 51 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 56 | The method, step by step: 9. **Deduct brought-forward losses**, oldest first, up to 75% of taxable income before loss relief (Article 37). ([Corporate Tax Law](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Federal-Decree-Law-No.-47-of-2022-and-its-amendments-en-v13.1.26.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 52 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 57 | The method, step by step: 10. **Apply the rates**: 0% on the first AED 375,000 of taxable income and 9% on the rest. A QFZP pays 0% on qualifying income and 9% on all other taxable income, with no AED 375,000 band. ([Corporate Tax Law](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Federal-Decree-Law-No.-47-of-2022-and-its-amendments-en-v13.1.26.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 53 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 58 | The method, step by step: 11. **Take credits** (foreign tax credit, capped at the UAE tax on the same income; withholding tax credit) and compute tax payable. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 54 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |

| ae-corporate-tax / base / 103 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: ### Qualifying Free Zone Persons ([Cabinet Decision No. 100 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%20100%20of%202023%20on%20Determining%20Qualifying%20Income%20for%20the%20Qualifying%20Free%20Zone%20Person%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf); [Ministerial Decision No. 229 of 2025](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2025/MD-No-229.pdf); [Free Zone Persons bulletin](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2024/CT%20Bulletin/Basic%20Tax%20Information%20bulletin-%20Free%20Zone%20Person-English.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 99 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 105 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: A Free Zone Person is a juridical person incorporated, established or registered in a free zone. Individuals and unincorporated partnerships cannot be Free Zone Persons. A Free Zone Person is a QFZP only while it meets **all** of these conditions (Corporate Tax Law, Article 18): | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 101 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 107 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: - It keeps adequate substance in the free zone or designated zone: core income-generating activities carried on there, with adequate assets, qualified full-time staff and operating costs. Outsourcing is allowed only within a free zone and under adequate supervision. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 103 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 108 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: - It derives Qualifying Income. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 104 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 109 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: - It has not elected to be taxed at the standard rates. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 105 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 110 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: - It complies with the arm's length principle and keeps transfer pricing documentation. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 106 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 111 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: - It prepares audited financial statements. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 107 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 112 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: - Its non-qualifying revenue does not exceed 5% of total revenue or AED 5,000,000, whichever is lower (the de minimis test in Ministerial Decision No. 229 of 2025, Article 3). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 108 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 114 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: **Qualifying Income** is income from transactions with other Free Zone Persons (who must be the beneficial recipients), except income from Excluded Activities. It also includes income from transactions with anyone for Qualifying Activities, qualifying intellectual property income, and other income while the de minimis test is met. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 110 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 116 | Qualifying Free Zone Persons: **Qualifying Activities** (MD 229 of 2025): manufacturing and processing of goods or materials; trading of qualifying commodities; holding shares and other securities for investment; ownership, management and operation of ships; reinsurance; fund management; wealth and investment management; headquarter services and treasury and financing services to related parties or for its own account; aircraft financing and leasing; distribution of goods or materials in or from a designated zone; logistics services; and ancillary activities. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 112 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-corporate-tax / base / 209 | Worked cases: ## Worked cases ([Corporate Tax Law, consolidated](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Federal-Decree-Law-No.-47-of-2022-and-its-amendments-en-v13.1.26.pdf); [Cabinet Decision No. 116 of 2022](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Corporate%20Tax/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%20116%20of%202022%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 59 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 211 | Worked cases: The amounts are illustrations. Each case uses a calendar-year tax period unless stated. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 207 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 213 | Worked cases: 1. **Freelance consultant, Small Business Relief.** | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 209 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 214 | Worked cases:    - **Facts:** a Dubai mainland sole establishment owner had business revenue of AED 1,400,000 in 2024; in 2025 it was AED 2,600,000 and in 2026 it was AED 2,900,000. Turnover passed AED 1,000,000 in 2024, so she was a Taxable Person and had to register by 31 March 2025. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 210 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 215 | Worked cases:    - **Relief:** revenue has been AED 3,000,000 or less in 2026 and in every earlier period, the 2026 period ends before 31 December 2029, and she is not in a multinational group. So she can elect Small Business Relief on her 2026 return, due 30 September 2027. Taxable income is treated as nil and the tax is nil. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 211 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 216 | Worked cases:    - **If she forgets to elect:** with taxable income of AED 900,000, tax is (AED 900,000 − AED 375,000) × 9% = AED 47,250 ([Cabinet Decision No. 116 of 2022](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Corporate%20Tax/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%20116%20of%202022%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 212 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 217 | Worked cases: 2. **Relief lost for good; standard computation.** | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 213 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 218 | Worked cases:    - **Facts:** an Abu Dhabi engineering LLC had revenue of AED 3,200,000 in 2025 and AED 4,200,000 in 2026. Its 2026 taxable income after all adjustments is AED 2,380,000. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 214 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 219 | Worked cases:    - **Tax:** (AED 2,380,000 − AED 375,000) × 9% = AED 180,450. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 215 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 220 | Worked cases:    - **Relief:** if revenue fell to AED 2,400,000 in 2027, Small Business Relief would still not be available, because 2025 revenue exceeded the threshold ([Ministerial Decision No. 73 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Corporate%20Tax/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%2073%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 216 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 221 | Worked cases: 3. **Loss cap and entertainment.** | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 217 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 222 | Worked cases:    - **Add-backs:** a company's 2026 accounting profit is AED 800,000. It includes AED 60,000 of client entertainment and an AED 5,000 traffic fine. Add back 50% of the entertainment (AED 30,000) and the whole fine: AED 800,000 + AED 30,000 + AED 5,000 = AED 835,000. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 218 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 223 | Worked cases:    - **Loss cap:** it has AED 700,000 of losses from 2024 and 2025. The cap is AED 835,000 × 75% = AED 626,250. Taxable income becomes AED 835,000 − AED 626,250 = AED 208,750, which falls inside the AED 375,000 0% band, so the tax is nil. The remaining AED 73,750 of losses carries forward. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 219 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 224 | Worked cases:    - **If it had elected Small Business Relief instead:** had its revenue allowed it to elect the relief in 2026, none of the AED 700,000 could be used in 2026, but all of it would survive for later non-relief years ([FTA losses bulletin](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2026/CT-Tax-Bulletin-Corporate-Tax-Losses-EN.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 220 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 225 | Worked cases: 4. **Interest limitation.** | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 221 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 226 | Worked cases:    - **Facts:** a property developer has net interest expenditure of AED 20,000,000 and tax-adjusted EBITDA of AED 50,000,000. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 222 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 227 | Worked cases:    - **Limit:** 30% of EBITDA is AED 50,000,000 × 30% = AED 15,000,000, which is higher than AED 12,000,000, so AED 15,000,000 is deductible. The other AED 5,000,000 carries forward for up to 10 periods ([Ministerial Decision No. 126 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2023/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%20126%20of%202023%20on%20the%20General%20Interest%20Deduction%20Limitation%20Rule%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 223 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 228 | Worked cases: 5. **QFZP that passes de minimis, with a mainland branch.** | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 224 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 229 | Worked cases:    - **De minimis test:** a free zone distributor has total revenue of AED 20,000,000 (excluding its mainland branch), all from Qualifying Activities except AED 800,000 of sales to individuals. The de minimis limit is the lower of AED 20,000,000 × 5% = AED 1,000,000 and AED 5,000,000, so it is AED 1,000,000. AED 800,000 does not exceed it, so the test is met. If it meets the other conditions, its qualifying income is taxed at 0%. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 225 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 230 | Worked cases:    - **Branch:** the branch's AED 500,000 of taxable income is taxed at 9% with no AED 375,000 band: AED 500,000 × 9% = AED 45,000. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 226 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 231 | Worked cases:    - **If the test had failed:** had sales to individuals been AED 1,200,000, it would fail and be taxed at the standard rates for 2026 and the four following periods ([Free Zone Persons bulletin](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2024/CT%20Bulletin/Basic%20Tax%20Information%20bulletin-%20Free%20Zone%20Person-English.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 227 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 232 | Worked cases: 6. **Free zone software house selling to the mainland.** | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 228 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 233 | Worked cases:    - **Facts:** a free zone software company earns AED 5,000,000, all from mainland companies. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 229 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 234 | Worked cases:    - **Outcome:** software development is not a Qualifying Activity, so all of that revenue is non-qualifying and far above the de minimis limit. It is not a QFZP. It pays 0% on the first AED 375,000 of taxable income and 9% above that, like a mainland company ([Ministerial Decision No. 229 of 2025](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2025/MD-No-229.pdf)). | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 230 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 235 | Worked cases: 7. **Late registration waived.** | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 231 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 236 | Worked cases:    - **Facts:** a company incorporated on 10 February 2026 with a 31 December year end had to register within 3 months, by 10 May 2026. It registered in August 2026, so the AED 10,000 late registration penalty applies. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 232 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 237 | Worked cases:    - **Waiver:** if it files its first return (period ending 31 December 2026) within 7 months, by 31 July 2027, the FTA waives the penalty, or refunds it to the EmaraTax account if it was paid. The tax itself is still due within 9 months, by 30 September 2027 ([CTP006](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2025/CTP006-Waiver-of-Late-Registration-Administrative-Penalty-02-07-2025.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 233 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-corporate-tax / base / 239 | Worked cases:    - **Freelancer:** a freelance designer with AED 750,000 of business turnover in 2026 is not subject to Corporate Tax and does not register. Monitor turnover, because once it exceeds AED 1,000,000 in a calendar year she must register by 31 March of the next year. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 235 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 240 | Worked cases:    - **Salaried landlord:** an employee who owns two flats and rents them out without a licence has no Corporate Tax at all. Wages and real estate investment income are not business income ([Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Legislation/05-06-2023/3/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2049%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 236 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-corporate-tax / base / 242 | Worked cases:    - **Facts:** the return for the year ended 31 December 2025 was due 30 September 2026 but was filed, with AED 100,000 of tax paid, on 15 December 2026. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 238 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 243 | Worked cases:    - **Late filing:** AED 500 is charged on 1 October, 1 November and 1 December: 3 × AED 500 = AED 1,500. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 239 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / base / 244 | Worked cases:    - **Late payment:** 14% a year, charged monthly for each month or part, on the unpaid tax. That is about AED 100,000 × 14% ÷ 12 × 3 = AED 3,500. EmaraTax computes the exact amount, so confirm it there ([Cabinet Decision No. 75 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2075%20of%202023%20and%20amendments%20-%20For%20Publishing.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 240 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 3 | frontmatter: description: "Use this skill whenever asked about UAE Corporate Tax for freelancers, sole establishments, or small businesses. Trigger on phrases like \"how much tax do I pay in UAE\", \"corporate tax UAE\", \"CT return\", \"FTA\", \"small business relief\", \"free zone tax\", \"qualifying free zone person\", \"AED 375,000\", \"9% tax\", \"taxable income UAE\", \"corporate tax registration\", \"UAE tax return\", \"self-employed tax UAE\", \"freelancer tax Dubai\", \"EmaraTax\", or any question about computing or filing UAE corporate tax. This skill covers the 0%/9% rate structure, small business relief (revenue under AED 3M), qualifying free zone person rules, deductible and non-deductible expenses, transfer pricing, registration requirements, and filing deadlines. Note: the UAE has NO personal income tax: self-employed individuals and sole establishments are subject to corporate tax. ALWAYS read this skill before touching any UAE corporate tax work." | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 3 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |

([Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Legislation/05-06-2023/3/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2049%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 35 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 35 | Ask the client first: - **Financial year end and first tax period.** This sets the return and payment deadline (9 months after the period ends). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 36 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 36 | Ask the client first: - **Revenue for this period and for every earlier tax period.** Small Business Relief fails permanently once any period's revenue has been over AED 3,000,000. | ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 37 | Ask the client first: - **Is the business part of a multinational group?** Ask for group consolidated revenue. Groups with EUR 750 million or more may be in scope of top-up tax. | ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 46 | The method, step by step: ## The method, step by step | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 42 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 48 | The method, step by step: 1. **Decide whether the person is a Taxable Person.** A juridical person resident in the UAE is always one, including a free zone company. A natural person is one only if their business turnover is more than AED 1,000,000 in the calendar year, not counting wages, personal investment income or real estate investment income. Check exemptions (government entities, qualifying public benefit entities, qualifying funds and others under Article 4) and refer them out. ([Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Legislation/05-06-2023/3/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2049%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 44 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 49 | The method, step by step: 2. **Check registration.** Every Taxable Person registers on EmaraTax by the deadline in FTA Decision No. 3 of 2024 (table below), including businesses that will claim Small Business Relief and free zone companies. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 45 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 50 | The method, step by step: 3. **Fix the tax period.** It is the financial year, meaning the Gregorian calendar year or the 12-month period for which financial statements are prepared (Article 57). Changing it needs an application to the FTA (Article 58). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 46 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 51 | The method, step by step: 4. **Test Small Business Relief** for a resident person. Revenue must be AED 3,000,000 or less in this period *and in every previous period*. The period must end on or before 31 December 2029. The person must not be a QFZP or a constituent company of a multinational group. If eligible and the client wants it, elect it on the return. Taxable income is then treated as nil and a simplified return is filed. ([Ministerial Decision No. 73 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Corporate%20Tax/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%2073%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 47 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 52 | The method, step by step: 5. **For a free zone company, test QFZP status**: adequate substance, qualifying income, the de minimis test, arm's length compliance with transfer pricing documentation, audited financial statements, and no election out. If any condition fails, it is taxed like a mainland company for that period and the next four. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 48 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 53 | The method, step by step: 6. **Compute taxable income** (Article 20): start from accounting net profit under IFRS (or IFRS for SMEs where allowed). Remove exempt income (for example qualifying dividends and participation income, Articles 22-23). Add back non-deductible items: 50% of entertainment, fines, bribes, owner drawings, Corporate Tax itself, recoverable input VAT, and donations to bodies that are not qualifying public benefit entities (Articles 28, 32, 33). Apportion mixed personal and business costs. ([Corporate Tax Law](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Federal-Decree-Law-No.-47-of-2022-and-its-amendments-en-v13.1.26.pdf)) | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; Article 20 base retained | Accounting/cash-basis eligibility and separate SBR MNE definition held; distinct master/local-file threshold retained. |
| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 54 | The method, step by step: 7. **Apply the interest limitation** if net interest expense is more than AED 12,000,000: the deduction is the higher of AED 12,000,000 and 30% of tax-adjusted EBITDA. The excess carries forward for 10 periods. ([Ministerial Decision No. 126 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2023/Ministerial%20Decision%20No.%20126%20of%202023%20on%20the%20General%20Interest%20Deduction%20Limitation%20Rule%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 50 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 56 | The method, step by step: 9. **Deduct brought-forward losses**, oldest first, up to 75% of taxable income before loss relief (Article 37). ([Corporate Tax Law](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Federal-Decree-Law-No.-47-of-2022-and-its-amendments-en-v13.1.26.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-corporate-tax.md line 52 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-corporate-tax / candidate-df33d21 / 223 | Worked cases:    - **Loss cap:** it has AED 700,000 of losses from 2024 and 2025. The cap is AED 835,000 × 75% = AED 626,250. | ae-payroll-social / base / 27 | Scope and who this is for: **Read this before relying on the social side.** The pension, labour and unemployment-insurance rules are made and published by the General Pension and Social Security Authority (GPSSA), the Abu Dhabi Pension Fund (for UAE nationals employed in Abu Dhabi), the Ministry of Human Resources and Emiratisation (MOHRE) and the federal legislation portal. This skill only cites the Federal Tax Authority and the Ministry of Finance. So it tells you **which** social rules apply and **what to confirm**, but it does not state pension rates, salary caps, payment days, gratuity day-counts or insurance premiums. Where you see "check", confirm the point with the body named before you run the payroll. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. | ae-payroll-social / base / 31 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: ## What UAE payroll does and does not involve ([Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023, Articles 1 and 2](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Legislation/05-06-2023/3/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2049%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf); [CTGTNP1, section 3.8.1](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Guides/CT/Taxation%20of%20natural%20persons%20-%2025%2011%202023.pdf); [CTGDTI1: Determination of Taxable Income](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Pdf/2024/Determination%20of%20Taxable%20Income%20-%2031%2007%202024.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 33 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 33 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **No tax on wages.** The UAE has no personal income tax. A natural person is only taxed on income under Corporate Tax, and only on business income; a natural person's Wage is outside it "regardless of the amount of Turnover". So there is nothing to calculate or withhold from an employee's pay for tax, and no employee tax return to file. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 35 | Explicit source/condition correction; all old wording retained in immutable snapshot. Broad PIT assertion is held rather than proved by the Wage exclusion. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 35 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **What counts as a wage.** The official definition is wide: "the wage that is given to the employee in consideration of their services under the employment contract, whether in cash or in kind, payable annually, monthly, weekly, daily, hourly, or by piece-meal, and includes all allowances, and bonuses in addition to any other benefits provided for, in the employment contract or in accordance with the applicable legislation in the State". Allowances, bonuses, benefits in kind and benefits due under UAE legislation (such as the statutory end-of-service gratuity) all fall inside it. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 37 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **No withholding tax either.** Corporate Tax law contains a withholding tax on certain payments to non-residents, but the FTA states "The current Withholding Tax rate applicable in the UAE is 0%", and the categories of income it would apply to have not been specified by Cabinet Decision. It does not reach wages. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 39 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 39 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **Employee or not is a question of fact.** The FTA says "whether a natural person is an employee and earns a salary or other form of remuneration as such, is a question of fact to be determined on a case-by-case basis". A person working under an employment contract is paid a Wage. A person who works for themselves and is not employed by the payer is running a Business, and if that person's business turnover exceeds AED 1,000,000 in a calendar year, that person must register for and pay Corporate Tax. The payer withholds nothing either way; the obligation is the contractor's own. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 41 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 41 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **Directors' fees.** "Generally, director fees will not be considered as a Business or Business Activity, and therefore would not be subject to Corporate Tax." Where a director is also an employee, the FTA's example treats both the salary and the board fees as a Wage. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 43 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-payroll-social / base / 67 | The method, step by step: ## The method, step by step | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 62 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 69 | The method, step by step: 1. Classify each person. Employment contract means Wage; no tax is withheld. Self-employed means the person handles their own Corporate Tax position, and you withhold nothing. If you cannot tell, treat it as a question of fact and look at the contract and how the work is done, not the label on the invoice ([CTGTNP1, section 3.8.1](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Guides/CT/Taxation%20of%20natural%20persons%20-%2025%2011%202023.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 64 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 70 | The method, step by step: 2. Sort employees by nationality. UAE nationals (and GCC nationals) go through the GPSSA pension process, or the Abu Dhabi Pension Fund for UAE nationals employed in Abu Dhabi (check); expatriates do not, and instead accrue the Labour Law end-of-service gratuity. Get the current rates, contribution salary basis, cap and monthly due date from the right fund before the first run. If you do this step after paying salaries, the employee share will not have been deducted and has to be recovered later. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. Original details remain in immutable snapshot. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 71 | The method, step by step: 3. Confirm the MOHRE obligations for the employer: whether it must pay through the Wage Protection System and on what timetable, and which employees must hold unemployment insurance. Free zone employers: check the free zone's own rules first. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. Original details remain in immutable snapshot. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 72 | The method, step by step: 4. Run payroll: gross pay, less the employee's pension share where it applies (check), less any other lawful deductions. No tax line. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. | ae-payroll-social / base / 123 | VAT on what the employer gives to staff: - **Staff accommodation.** From 1 October 2026 the labour-law exception no longer covers accommodation unless a MOHRE decision or directive makes it mandatory. Whether a contract or policy can still bring accommodation within exception (2) depends on the "cases and conditions" the FTA specifies. As of 25 September 2026 this skill has not seen those conditions on the FTA site; treat recovery on accommodation supplied from 1 October 2026 as unsettled and check the FTA's published guidance before claiming it. The block only matters where the accommodation carries VAT, such as a hotel stay or a hotel or serviced apartment, which are not "residential buildings" (Executive Regulation, Article 37(2)). A lease of a residential building is exempt "where the lease is more than 6 six months or the tenant of the property is a holder of an ID card issued by Federal Authority for Identity and Citizenship" (Article 43(1)), so an ordinary long staff flat lease has no input tax to block. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 102 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 124 | VAT on what the employer gives to staff: - **Contract or policy benefits generally.** The old test (needed for the role, and normal business practice) is replaced by the FTA's own cases and conditions. Check them before claiming. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 103 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 125 | VAT on what the employer gives to staff: - **Health insurance.** Recovery continues for the employee, one spouse and up to three children under 18. Input tax on cover for a fourth child, or a child aged 18 or over, falls outside exception (3) and is blocked unless another exception applies. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 110 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 126 | VAT on what the employer gives to staff: - **Entertainment of non-staff.** Input tax on entertainment for customers, potential customers, officials, shareholders or investors stays blocked for non-government employers. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 111 | Exact text retained. |

The changes to the input-tax apportionment rules in the same Decision start later, from the first tax year commencing after 1 October 2027; they are not payroll rules and are covered in `uae-vat`. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 113 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 130 | Corporate Tax on payroll costs for the employer: ## Corporate Tax on payroll costs for the employer ([CTGDTI1, sections 4.5.2 and 4.5.10](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Pdf/2024/Determination%20of%20Taxable%20Income%20-%2031%2007%202024.pdf); [Ministerial Decision No. 115 of 2023, Article 5](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/Ministerial-Decision-No.115-of-2023-on-Private-Pension-Funds-and-Private-Social-Security-Funds-for-Corporate-Tax-Purposes.pdf); [CTGEPF1: Pension Funds and Social Security Funds](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Guides/CT/Exempt%20Persons%20-%20Public%20Benefit%20Entities%20Pension%20Funds%20and%20Social%20Security%20Funds%20-%2001%2012%202023.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 115 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 132 | Corporate Tax on payroll costs for the employer: - **Pay and benefits are deductible.** "Employee costs are generally considered to be wholly and exclusively incurred for Business purposes provided that they are not excessive". It does not matter whether an employee is paid in cash or in benefits such as a car, medical insurance or a flight allowance for family. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 117 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 133 | Corporate Tax on payroll costs for the employer: - **Related-party pay is tested at Market Value.** Salary or bonus paid to directors, officers or owners "would be deductible ... but only insofar as this salary corresponds with the Market Value rates for such services rendered". Any excess is added back. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 118 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 134 | Corporate Tax on payroll costs for the employer: - **Staff events are not entertainment.** Staff parties, away-days and performance rewards are employee costs and fully deductible. A private event, such as a family wedding for relatives who happen to work there, is not. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 119 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 135 | Corporate Tax on payroll costs for the employer: - **Private pension fund contributions.** An employer "may deduct the total value of contributions made to a private pension fund in respect of its employees who are Pension Plan Members in the Tax Period in which such contributions are paid", capped at 15% of that member's total deductible remuneration for the Tax Period. Contributions not paid in the period, or above the cap, are added back. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 120 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 136 | Corporate Tax on payroll costs for the employer: - **Funds that hold the gratuity.** A private social security fund is "a fund created by a private employer for the purposes of providing statutory end of service gratuity payments to employees". Such a fund, and a private pension fund, can apply to the FTA to be an Exempt Person if it meets the conditions. Public pension and social security funds can also apply for exemption. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 121 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-payroll-social / base / 154 | Worked cases: **Case 1: expatriate employee on the mainland.** A Dubai mainland company employs an expatriate on a monthly salary with housing and transport allowances. The company accrues the Labour Law end-of-service gratuity each month (check the day-count and basis with MOHRE), pays through the Wage Protection System if it applies (check), and confirms the employee holds unemployment insurance (check). The salary and allowances are deductible in the company's Corporate Tax return if not excessive. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. This skill gives no rate because it cannot cite GPSSA. | ae-payroll-social / base / 158 | Worked cases: **Case 3: private pension fund contributions over the cap.** In its 2026 Tax Period a company pays AED 120,000 into a private pension fund for one employee who is a Pension Plan Member. The employee's total deductible remuneration for the period is AED 600,000. The cap is 15% of AED 600,000 = AED 90,000. The company deducts AED 90,000 and adds back AED 30,000 (AED 120,000 less AED 90,000). Had any of the AED 120,000 been accrued but not paid in the period, that part would also be added back for this period. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 143 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 160 | Worked cases: **Case 4: consultant paid by invoice.** A company engages a self-employed consultant who invoices AED 1,200,000 in calendar year 2026 and has no employment contract. The payments are not a Wage. The company withholds nothing. The consultant's business turnover exceeds AED 1,000,000, so the consultant must register for and pay Corporate Tax on their own account (route the consultant to the `ae-income-tax` skill). The company deducts the fees as a business expense, at Market Value if the consultant is a related party. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 145 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | ae-payroll-social / base / 162 | Worked cases: **Case 5: health insurance and accommodation, VAT-registered employer.** An employer buys family health cover for an employee with a spouse and four children (ages 4, 9, 14 and 19) and, from October 2026, pays for a serviced apartment for the employee. Input tax on the cover for the employee, spouse and the three children under 18 is recoverable under exception (3). The cover for the 19-year-old is outside exception (3), so its input tax is blocked unless another exception applies. A serviced apartment is not a "residential building" for VAT, so its charge carries VAT. Input tax on the October 2026 serviced-apartment charge is blocked unless a MOHRE decision or directive makes that accommodation mandatory, or the case falls within the contract-or-policy cases the FTA specifies; until those cases are published, do not claim it without checking. Had the employer instead leased an ordinary residential flat for more than six months (or to a tenant holding an Emirates ID card), the lease would be exempt: there would be no input tax on the rent, and the Article 53 change would not arise. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 147 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / base / 164 | Worked cases: **Case 6: executive director with board fees.** An employee who is a senior manager also sits on the board and receives board-meeting fees. Following the FTA's own example, both the salary and the fees are typically a Wage, so neither is taxed. If the director is also a shareholder, the company deducts the pay only to the extent it matches Market Value. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 151 | Exact text retained. |

| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 32 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: ## What UAE payroll does and does not involve ([Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2023, Articles 1 and 2](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Legislation/05-06-2023/3/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2049%20of%202023%20-%20for%20publishing.pdf); [CTGTNP1, section 3.8.1](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Guides/CT/Taxation%20of%20natural%20persons%20-%2025%2011%202023.pdf); [CTGDTI1: Determination of Taxable Income](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Pdf/2024/Determination%20of%20Taxable%20Income%20-%2031%2007%202024.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 33 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 34 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **No tax on wages.** The UAE has no personal income tax. A natural person is only taxed on income under Corporate Tax, and only on business income; a natural person's Wage is outside it "regardless of the amount of Turnover". So there is nothing to calculate or withhold from an employee's pay for tax, and no employee tax return to file. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 35 | Explicit source/condition correction; all old wording retained in immutable snapshot. Broad PIT assertion is held rather than proved by the Wage exclusion. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 36 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **What counts as a wage.** The official definition is wide: "the wage that is given to the employee in consideration of their services under the employment contract, whether in cash or in kind, payable annually, monthly, weekly, daily, hourly, or by piece-meal, and includes all allowances, and bonuses in addition to any other benefits provided for, in the employment contract or in accordance with the applicable legislation in the State". Allowances, bonuses, benefits in kind and benefits due under UAE legislation (such as the statutory end-of-service gratuity) all fall inside it. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 38 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **No withholding tax either.** Corporate Tax law contains a withholding tax on certain payments to non-residents, but the FTA states "The current Withholding Tax rate applicable in the UAE is 0%", and the categories of income it would apply to have not been specified by Cabinet Decision. It does not reach wages. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 39 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 40 | What UAE payroll does and does not involve: **Employee or not is a question of fact.** The FTA says "whether a natural person is an employee and earns a salary or other form of remuneration as such, is a question of fact to be determined on a case-by-case basis". A person working under an employment contract is paid a Wage. A person who works for themselves and is not employed by the payer is running a Business, and if that person's business turnover exceeds AED 1,000,000 in a calendar year, that person must register for and pay Corporate Tax. The payer withholds nothing either way; the obligation is the contractor's own. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 41 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |

| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 70 | The method, step by step: 1. Classify each person. Employment contract means Wage; no tax is withheld. Self-employed means the person handles their own Corporate Tax position, and you withhold nothing. If you cannot tell, treat it as a question of fact and look at the contract and how the work is done, not the label on the invoice ([CTGTNP1, section 3.8.1](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Guides/CT/Taxation%20of%20natural%20persons%20-%2025%2011%202023.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 64 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 71 | The method, step by step: 2. Sort employees by nationality. UAE nationals (and GCC nationals) go through the GPSSA pension process, or the Abu Dhabi Pension Fund for UAE nationals employed in Abu Dhabi (check); expatriates do not, and instead accrue the Labour Law end-of-service gratuity. Get the current rates, contribution salary basis, cap and monthly due date from the right fund before the first run. If you do this step after paying salaries, the employee share will not have been deducted and has to be recovered later. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. Original details remain in immutable snapshot. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 72 | The method, step by step: 3. Confirm the MOHRE obligations for the employer: whether it must pay through the Wage Protection System and on what timetable, and which employees must hold unemployment insurance. Free zone employers: check the free zone's own rules first. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. Original details remain in immutable snapshot. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 73 | The method, step by step: 4. Run payroll: gross pay, less the employee's pension share where it applies (check), less any other lawful deductions. No tax line. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. Accrue the end-of-service gratuity for expatriate staff each month so the liability is in the accounts when the person leaves. Confirm the day-count and the pay basis with MOHRE or the free zone. | SPLIT / NON-OPERATIVE HOLD: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md Sources / Not yet confirmed; social coverage hold; tax components retained in corresponding method/case | Unconfirmed social scope/routing/accrual/execution is not asserted as law. | ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 124 | VAT on what the employer gives to staff: - **Staff accommodation.** From 1 October 2026 the labour-law exception no longer covers accommodation unless a MOHRE decision or directive makes it mandatory. Under exception (2), recovery needs a contractual obligation or documented policy and all the conditions of case 3 or case 4 in the list below. The block only matters where the accommodation carries VAT, such as a hotel stay or a hotel or serviced apartment, which are not "residential buildings" (Executive Regulation, Article 37(2)). | ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 165 | Worked cases: **Case 3: private pension fund contributions over the cap.** In its 2026 Tax Period a company pays AED 120,000 into a private pension fund for one employee who is a Pension Plan Member. The employee's total deductible remuneration for the period is AED 600,000. The cap is 15% of AED 600,000 = AED 90,000. The company deducts AED 90,000 and adds back AED 30,000 (AED 120,000 less AED 90,000). Had any of the AED 120,000 been accrued but not paid in the period, that part would also be added back for this period. | RETAINED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 143 | Exact text retained. |
| ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 167 | Worked cases: **Case 4: consultant paid by invoice.** A company engages a self-employed consultant who invoices AED 1,200,000 in calendar year 2026 and has no employment contract. The payments are not a Wage. The company withholds nothing. The consultant's business turnover exceeds AED 1,000,000, so the consultant must register for and pay Corporate Tax on their own account (route the consultant to the `ae-income-tax` skill). The company deducts the fees as a business expense, at Market Value if the consultant is a related party. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 145 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | ae-payroll-social / candidate-df33d21 / 169 | Worked cases: **Case 5: health insurance and accommodation, VAT-registered employer.** An employer buys family health cover for an employee with a spouse and four children (ages 4, 9, 14 and 19) and, from October 2026, pays for a serviced apartment for the employee. Input tax on the cover for the employee, spouse and the three children under 18 is recoverable under exception (3). The cover for the 19-year-old is outside exception (3), so its input tax is blocked unless another exception applies. A serviced apartment is not a "residential building" for VAT, so its charge carries VAT. Input tax on the October 2026 serviced-apartment charge is blocked unless a MOHRE decision or directive makes that accommodation mandatory, or a contract or documented policy provides it and every condition of case 3 of FTA Decision No. 17 of 2026 is met (or case 4, for a new employee, up to 30 days). If the employee could take a housing allowance instead, or the apartment is part of the ordinary package, it fails case 3. Had the employer instead leased an ordinary residential flat for more than six months (or to a tenant holding an Emirates ID card), the lease would be exempt: there would be no input tax on the rent, and the Article 53 change would not arise. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/ae-payroll-social.md line 147 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | uae-vat / base / 3 | frontmatter: description: Use this skill whenever asked to prepare, review, or classify transactions for a UAE VAT return (VAT201 form) for any client. Trigger on phrases like "prepare VAT return", "do the VAT", "fill in VAT201", "create the return", "UAE VAT filing", "FTA return", or any request involving UAE VAT filing. Also trigger when classifying transactions for VAT purposes from bank statements, invoices, or other source data. This skill covers the UAE only and only standard VAT-registered persons filing VAT201. VAT groups, profit margin schemes, partial exemption with non-trivial exempt supplies, and Designated Zone goods movement classifications are all in the refusal catalogue. MUST be loaded alongside vat-workflow-base v0.1 or later (for workflow architecture). ALWAYS read this skill before touching any UAE VAT work. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 3 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / base / 15 | UAE VAT: 2026 registration, rates, VAT201 return, reverse charge, input tax and e-invoicing: # UAE VAT: 2026 registration, rates, VAT201 return, reverse charge, input tax and e-invoicing | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 16 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 17 | UAE VAT: 2026 registration, rates, VAT201 return, reverse charge, input tax and e-invoicing: Tax year 2026. UAE VAT is filed by tax period (usually a quarter), not by annual return, so "2026" here means tax periods that fall in calendar year 2026. The rules below are the law as published on tax.gov.ae and mof.gov.ae on 25 September 2026. Two sets of changes took effect this year (1 January 2026 and 14 April 2026) and one more takes effect on 1 October 2026; each is dated where it applies. A short section covers returns for periods that ended before those dates. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 18 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 19 | Scope and who this is for: ## Scope and who this is for | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 20 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 21 | Scope and who this is for: Sources for this section: [VAT Law, consolidated to Decree-Law No. 16 of 2025](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf); [Executive Regulation, consolidated 09-2026](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 22 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 23 | Scope and who this is for: This skill covers federal Value Added Tax under Federal Decree-Law No. 8 of 2017 ("VAT Law") and its Executive Regulation (Cabinet Decision No. 52 of 2017), for: | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 24 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 25 | Scope and who this is for: - businesses deciding whether they must or may register; | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 26 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 26 | Scope and who this is for: - a single VAT-registered business (not a Tax Group) preparing or reviewing its VAT201 return; | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 27 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 27 | Scope and who this is for: - classifying sales and purchases: standard rate, zero rate, exempt, not a supply, reverse charge, blocked input tax; | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 28 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 28 | Scope and who this is for: - invoicing, record keeping, penalties and the e-invoicing timeline. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 29 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 30 | Scope and who this is for: It does not compute partial-exemption apportionment, Tax Group returns, Designated Zone goods movements, capital asset scheme adjustments, the oil and gas reverse charge, or tourist refund scheme entries. Those are referred out (see "When to refuse or refer"). It does not cover Corporate Tax or Excise Tax. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 31 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 32 | Scope and who this is for: The standard rate is 5% ([VAT Law, Article 3](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)). All amounts on the return are in UAE dirhams (AED). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 33 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 34 | Ask the client first: ## Ask the client first | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 35 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 36 | Ask the client first: - **Is the business registered, and from what date?** Get the TRN, the effective date of registration and the tax period on the registration certificate. The period the FTA assigned governs, not a rule of thumb. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 37 | Ask the client first: - **Is it a single registrant or a member of a Tax Group?** Tax Groups are referred out. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 38 | Ask the client first: - **Where is it established?** Which Emirate (for the Box 1 split), and is it in a Designated Zone, a free zone that is not designated, or the mainland? | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 39 | Ask the client first: - **What does it sell, and to whom?** Local customers, customers abroad, other GCC countries, consumers or businesses. Any residential property, bare land, financial services, education, healthcare or local passenger transport? | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 38 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 40 | Ask the client first: - **Does it make any exempt supplies?** If more than trivial, input tax must be apportioned: refer. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 38 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 41 | Ask the client first: - **What did it buy from abroad?** Services or software billed by a supplier with no UAE TRN (reverse charge), and goods imported through UAE customs (Box 6). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 39 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 42 | Ask the client first: - **Which costs are entertainment, cars, or staff benefits?** These are the main blocked items. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 39 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 43 | Ask the client first: - **Has it paid its suppliers?** Input tax on an unpaid bill is claimable only if the business intends to pay within six months of the agreed payment date ([Executive Regulation, Article 54(2)](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 40 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 44 | Ask the client first: - **Any errors in earlier returns?** The size of the tax difference decides whether a voluntary disclosure is needed. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 41 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 45 | Ask the client first: - **Excess input tax carried forward?** And from which tax period it arose (the five-year limit applies from 1 January 2026). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 41 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 46 | Ask the client first: - **Revenue in the last accounting period?** It decides the e-invoicing deadline (the AED 50,000,000 test in [Ministerial Decision No. 244 of 2025](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/Ministerial-Decision-No.-244-of-2025-on-the-Implementation-of-the-Electronic-Invoicing-System.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 42 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 48 | The method, step by step: ## The method, step by step | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 44 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 50 | The method, step by step: 1. **Confirm registration.** If not registered, test the mandatory threshold (supplies in the last 12 months, or expected in the next 30 days) and the voluntary threshold ([Executive Regulation, Articles 7 and 8](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). A late registrant owes VAT on supplies made from the date it should have been registered. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 46 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 51 | The method, step by step: 2. **Fix the tax period and deadline.** Take the period from the certificate. The return and the payment must both reach the FTA by the 28th day after the period ends. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 47 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 52 | The method, step by step: 3. **Classify each sale.** Standard 5%, zero-rated, exempt, or not a supply ([VAT Law, Articles 3, 45 and 46](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)). Check the zero-rating conditions. Services to an overseas customer are zero-rated only if all of these hold: the customer has no place of residence in a GCC implementing state; the customer is outside the UAE when the services are performed; the services are not directly connected with real estate in the UAE, or with moveable goods in the UAE at the time the services are performed; and the services are not treated as performed in the UAE under the special place-of-supply rules in VAT Law Articles 30(3)-(8) and 31. Services actually performed outside the implementing states are also zero-rated ([Executive Regulation, Article 31(1)](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 48 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 53 | The method, step by step: 4. **Split standard-rated sales by Emirate** for Box 1a-1g. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 49 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 54 | The method, step by step: 5. **Pick up reverse-charge purchases.** Services and goods from suppliers with no UAE place of residence who are not charging UAE VAT go in Box 3 (output VAT) and, if recoverable, Box 10. Imports through UAE customs appear in Box 6; correct them in Box 7. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 50 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 55 | The method, step by step: 6. **Classify each purchase.** Keep only input tax on costs used for taxable supplies, backed by a tax invoice (or import documents), and paid or intended to be paid within six months of the agreed payment date. Strip out blocked items: entertainment of non-employees, motor vehicles available for personal use, and free personal benefits to staff. Conservative defaults where the facts are missing: an item whose blocked status is unknown is treated as blocked; a purchase whose VAT status is unknown is not recovered; if the Emirate of a sale is unknown, ask before filing. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 51 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 56 | The method, step by step: 7. **Deal with credit notes and past errors.** Credit notes reduce the relevant box. Where a past return understated the payable tax: an error of AED 10,000 or less in tax is corrected in the return; above that, file a voluntary disclosure within 20 business days. An error in the business's favour (tax overstated) may be corrected by voluntary disclosure but need not be ([Cabinet Decision No. 74 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2074%20of%202023%20on%20Executive%20Regulation%20of%20Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%2028%20of%202022%20on%20Tax%20Procedures%20-%20For%20Publishing.pdf)). | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 52 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| uae-vat / base / 57 | The method, step by step: 8. **Compute the return.** Box 12 (due tax) less Box 13 (recoverable tax) gives Box 14. If Box 14 is negative, choose a refund or carry the excess forward, noting the five-year limit. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 53 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 58 | The method, step by step: 9. **File and pay** through the FTA's online portal by the 28th day. Keep the records for at least five years. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 54 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / base / 67 | Registration: \| When it bites \| A person with a place of residence in the UAE or another GCC implementing state must register where the supplies counted under Article 19 in the previous 12 months **exceeded** the threshold, or are expected to exceed it in the next 30 days \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 63 | Exact text retained. |

Either way, whether or not the person applies \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 65 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 70 | Registration: \| Non-resident \| Must register if making supplies in the UAE where no other person is obliged to pay the VAT; registered from the date it started making supplies; no threshold \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 66 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / base / 72 | Registration: \| Voluntary test \| Supplies **or taxable expenses** in the previous 12 months exceeded the voluntary threshold (shown at the end of any month), or are expected to in the next 30 days. "Taxable expenses" means standard-rated expenses incurred in the UAE by a person with a place of residence in the UAE. The person must also prove it carries on a business in the UAE and intends to make taxable (or equivalent) supplies. Registration takes effect from the first day of the month after the application, or an earlier agreed date \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 68 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 73 | Registration: \| Late registration \| VAT is due on all taxable supplies and imports made before registering \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 69 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 75 | Registration: "Exceeded" means more than the threshold; supplies of exactly AED 375,000 do not trigger mandatory registration. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 71 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / base / 91 | Tax period and deadline: - The standard tax period is three calendar months, ending on the date the FTA sets. The FTA may assign a shorter (for example monthly) or longer period, and a business on the standard period may ask for its quarters to end in a month it chooses. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 87 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / base / 95 | Excess recoverable tax: ### Excess recoverable tax ([VAT Law, Article 74](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf); [Tax Procedures Law, consolidated](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%2028%20of%202022%20-%20publishing%2003%2012%202025.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 91 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 97 | Excess recoverable tax: - The FTA first offsets excess recoverable tax against tax and penalties due. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 93 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 98 | Excess recoverable tax: - If no refund is requested, the excess is carried forward for no more than five years from the end of the tax period in which it arose. After that the right lapses and it cannot be used to settle any liability. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 94 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 99 | Excess recoverable tax: - A refund application must be made within five years from the end of the relevant tax period. Transitional rule: where that five-year period had already run out, the taxpayer may still apply for the refund, or use the balance, within one year from 1 January 2026. A voluntary disclosure linked to such a refund application must be made within two years of the application. Check the facts before relying on it. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 95 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | uae-vat / base / 125 | The VAT201 return, box by box: - Supplier bad debt relief ([VAT Law, Article 64(2)](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)): the buyer must reduce its recoverable input tax only when all of these hold: the supplier has written off the debt, reduced its output tax and notified the buyer; the buyer deducted the input tax; and the consideration has been unpaid, in full or in part, for over 6 months.
