| uae-vat / base / 157 | Input tax recovery and blocked items: **Change from 1 October 2026** ([Cabinet Decision No. 149 of 2026](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Cabinet-Decision-No.-149-of-2026-Amending-Certain-Provisions-of-The-Executive-Regulation-of-VAT-EN.pdf)): the labour-law exception excludes staff accommodation unless the Ministry of Human Resources and Emiratisation makes it mandatory; the contract-or-policy exception applies "in accordance with the cases and conditions specified by the Authority" (until 30 September 2026 the goods or services must be provided "in order that they may perform their role" and this must be shown to be normal business practice, [Executive Regulation as at October 2025](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/Cabinet-Decision-No.-52-of-2017-of-the-Executive-Regulation-of-the-Federal-Decree-Law-No.-8-of-2017-on-Value-Added-Tax-and-its-amendments.pdf)). No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 166 | Deemed supplies and gifts: - A deemed supply is reported in Box 1 and needs a tax invoice (delivered to the recipient, or kept on file if there is none). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 173 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 168 | Designated Zones: ## Designated Zones ([Executive Regulation, Article 51](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf); [VAT Law, Articles 51 and 52](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 175 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 170 | Designated Zones: - A Designated Zone is a zone named by Cabinet decision that is fenced, has customs controls and has internal procedures for goods. Only then is it treated as outside the UAE. Not every free zone is a Designated Zone: check the current Cabinet list. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 177 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 171 | Designated Zones: - Services supplied in a Designated Zone are treated as supplied inside the UAE: 5% as normal. Water and energy too. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 181 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 172 | Designated Zones: - Goods moved between Designated Zones are not taxed if they are not released, used or altered in transit and move under customs suspension. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 179 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 173 | Designated Zones: - Goods supplied within a Designated Zone to be consumed there are supplied inside the UAE, unless incorporated into another good in the same zone that is not consumed, or evidenced as removed from the UAE or imported with VAT paid. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 180 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 174 | Designated Zones: - A person established or registered in a Designated Zone is treated as resident in the UAE. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 181 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 175 | Designated Zones: - Goods movements into, out of and between zones are referred out. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 182 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 177 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: ## Tax invoices and simplified invoices ([VAT Law, Articles 65, 67 and 69](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf); [Executive Regulation, Article 59](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 184 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 179 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: - A registrant issues a tax invoice within 14 days of the date of supply. Amounts in a foreign currency are converted to AED at the UAE Central Bank rate on the date of supply. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 186 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| uae-vat / base / 180 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: - **Full tax invoice:** the words "Tax Invoice"; supplier's name, address and TRN; recipient's name, address and TRN if registered; sequential or unique number; date of issue; date of supply if different; description; unit price, quantity, rate and amount in AED for each item; any discount; gross amount in AED; VAT in AED with the exchange rate used; and, for a reverse-charge supply, a statement that the recipient must account for the tax with the legal reference. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 187 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 181 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: - **Simplified tax invoice** (the words "Tax Invoice", supplier name, address and TRN, date, description, total and VAT in AED) is allowed where the customer is not registered, or the customer is registered and the consideration does not exceed AED 10,000. It is not allowed for reverse-charge supplies. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 188 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 182 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: - A wholly zero-rated supply needs no tax invoice if records are sufficient. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 189 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 183 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: - Buyer-created ("Tax Invoice raised by buyer") invoices are allowed if the buyer is registered and both parties agree in writing. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 190 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| uae-vat / base / 184 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: - A tax credit note must show the words "Tax Credit Note" (the exact wording set by Cabinet Decision No. 149 of 2026 from 1 October 2026). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 191 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 185 | Tax invoices and simplified invoices: - Where the business is within the e-invoicing system, tax invoices must be issued and transmitted as electronic invoices ([VAT Law, Article 65(5)](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 192 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 187 | Profit margin scheme: ## Profit margin scheme ([Executive Regulation, Article 29](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf); [FTA Profit Margin Scheme Guide VATGPM1](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Pdf/2026/Guide/Profit%20Margin-Scheme-EN-02-01-2026-re.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 194 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 189 | Profit margin scheme: - Available for second-hand goods, antiques (over 50 years old) and collectors' items that have already borne VAT, bought from a non-registrant or from a seller who used the margin scheme; also for goods on which input tax was blocked under Executive Regulation Article 53. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 196 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 190 | Profit margin scheme: - VAT is charged on the margin (selling price less purchase price), which is treated as VAT-inclusive. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 197 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 191 | Profit margin scheme: - Not available if the invoice for the sale shows an amount of VAT. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 198 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| uae-vat / base / 192 | Profit margin scheme: - The seller keeps a stock book and purchase invoices (self-issued where bought from a non-registrant) and issues a tax invoice stating that VAT was charged on the margin, without showing the VAT amount. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 199 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 193 | Profit margin scheme: - The purchase price includes costs or fees incurred to buy the goods. From 1 October 2026 it includes only those costs or fees whose input tax is not recoverable, which narrows the purchase price and so can increase the margin. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 200 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 194 | Profit margin scheme: - The business must notify the FTA that it uses the scheme; failing to is a penalty of AED 2,500. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 201 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / base / 243 | Worked cases: Sources for this section: [VAT Law, consolidated](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf); [Executive Regulation, consolidated](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf); [FTA VAT Returns User Guide](https://tax.gov.ae/DataFolder/Files/Pdf/VAT%20Returns%20User%20GuideEnglishV40%2015%2008%202021%20SEP2021.pdf). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 251 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 245 | Worked cases: **Case 1: mandatory registration.** A Dubai mainland trading LLC, not registered, has taxable supplies of AED 390,000 in the 12 months to 30 June 2026. That exceeds AED 375,000, so it must apply within 30 days of becoming required to register. The FTA registers it from the first day of the month after the month in which it became required to register, whether or not it applies, and it owes VAT on its taxable supplies from that date. A late application is a penalty of AED 10,000. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 253 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 247 | Worked cases: **Case 2: voluntary registration.** A start-up has no sales yet but taxable expenses of AED 200,000 in the last 12 months. That exceeds AED 187,500, so it may register voluntarily and recover input tax. It is not required to register. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 255 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 249 | Worked cases: **Case 3: reverse charge on software.** A registered Dubai consultancy pays a US software company AED 3,672 for a subscription. The supplier has no UAE TRN and charges no VAT. Box 3: amount AED 3,672, VAT 3,672 x 5% = AED 183.60. Box 10: the same amounts, because the consultancy makes only taxable supplies. Net effect nil. For a supply received after 1 January 2026 no self-invoice is issued; the supplier's invoice is kept. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 257 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | uae-vat / base / 298 | Worked cases: **Case 5: gift limit.** In the 12 months to 31 August 2026 a business gives commercial gifts of goods on which it recovered input tax. One customer receives goods costing AED 400: no deemed supply (not above AED 500). Another receives goods costing AED 700: that is a deemed supply reported in Box 1, subject to the AED 2,000 relief on total output tax on deemed supplies in the 12 months. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 323 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 300 | Worked cases: **Case 6: correcting an error.** In September 2026 a business finds it under-declared output tax of AED 12,000 in the quarter to 31 March 2026. That is above AED 10,000, so it files a voluntary disclosure within 20 business days of finding the error. If the difference had been AED 8,000 it would correct it in the next return instead. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 325 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 302 | When to refuse or refer: ## When to refuse or refer | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 327 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 304 | When to refuse or refer: - **Tax Group members:** consolidation and intra-group supplies need a registered tax agent. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 329 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 305 | When to refuse or refer: - **Partial exemption:** any business with more than incidental exempt supplies (residential property, margin-based financial services) needs apportionment of mixed input tax, and a new method applies from the first tax year beginning after 1 October 2027. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 330 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 306 | When to refuse or refer: - **Designated Zone goods movements:** goods entering, leaving or moving between Designated Zones. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 331 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 307 | When to refuse or refer: - **Capital asset scheme:** assets costing AED 5,000,000 or more (excluding VAT), on which VAT is payable, with a useful life of 10 years or more for buildings, 5 years or more otherwise ([Executive Regulation, Article 57](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 332 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 308 | When to refuse or refer: - **Oil, gas and hydrocarbons** between registrants (domestic reverse charge) and other special reverse charges. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 333 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 309 | When to refuse or refer: - **Tourist Refund Scheme** operators or retailers. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 334 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 310 | When to refuse or refer: - **Complex profit margin scheme** cases (mixed stock, second-hand vehicles, partial exports). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 335 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 311 | When to refuse or refer: - **Tax evasion chain notices** from the FTA, audits, objections and reconsideration requests. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 336 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 312 | When to refuse or refer: - **Penalties for violations that began before 14 April 2026:** the decision text we found does not say whether the new table applies to them; check with the FTA. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 337 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 313 | When to refuse or refer: - **Cash-payment input tax block** from 1 October 2026, until the Minister's decision setting the value is found. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 338 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 314 | When to refuse or refer: - **GCC implementing-state supplies** (goods or services to or from other GCC countries). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 339 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 316 | Filing and payment: ## Filing and payment | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 341 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 318 | Returns and payment: ### Returns and payment ([Executive Regulation, Article 64](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 343 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 320 | Returns and payment: - File the VAT201 return online through the FTA's portal for every tax period, including nil periods. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 345 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 321 | Returns and payment: - The return and the payment must reach the FTA no later than the 28th day after the end of the tax period, or such other date as the FTA directs (for a quarter ending 30 September 2026, by 28 October 2026). There is no separate annual VAT return. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 346 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 322 | Returns and payment: - For the late payment penalty, tax shown in a voluntary disclosure is due 20 business days after submission, and tax in an FTA assessment 20 business days after receipt. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 347 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 324 | Returns being filed now for earlier periods: ### Returns being filed now for earlier periods ([VAT Law, consolidated](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 349 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / base / 326 | Returns being filed now for earlier periods: - A tax period that ended before 1 January 2026 follows the VAT Law before Decree-Law No. 16 of 2025, including the self-invoice requirement for reverse-charge supplies in that period. Correct past errors on the rules of the period concerned. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 351 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 327 | Returns being filed now for earlier periods: - Penalties for a return or payment due before 14 April 2026 may fall under the earlier table (Cabinet Decision No. 49 of 2021); check. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 352 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 328 | Returns being filed now for earlier periods: - Excess recoverable tax that arose in earlier periods is now subject to the five-year limit; check the age of any carried-forward balance. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 353 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 330 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: ### Penalties from 14 April 2026 ([Cabinet Decision No. 40 of 2017 as amended by Cabinet Decision No. 129 of 2025](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2040%20of%202017%20and%20its%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2011%202025.pdf)) | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 355 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 332 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Violation \| Penalty (AED) \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 357 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 333 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| --- \| --- \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 61 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 334 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Late registration application \| 10,000 \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 359 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 335 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Late deregistration application \| 1,000 on the same date each month, up to 10,000 \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 360 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 336 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Late return \| 1,000 first time; 2,000 if repeated within 24 months \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 361 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 337 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Late payment \| Monthly penalty at 14% per annum for each month or part of a month on the unpaid tax, from the day after the due date and on the same date each month \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 362 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 338 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Incorrect return \| 500, unless corrected within the filing deadline or by a voluntary disclosure that does not change the tax due \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 363 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 339 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Voluntary disclosure of an error \| 1% of the tax difference per month or part of a month, from the day after the original return's due date to the disclosure \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 364 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 340 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| No voluntary disclosure before notice of audit \| Fixed 15% of the tax difference plus 1% per month or part of a month \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 365 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 341 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Failure to keep records \| 10,000; 20,000 if repeated within 24 months \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 366 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 342 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Failure to give records in Arabic when asked \| 5,000 \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 367 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 343 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Failure to notify changes to the tax record \| 1,000; 5,000 if repeated within 24 months \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 368 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 344 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Failure to display VAT-inclusive prices \| 5,000 \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 369 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 345 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Failure to notify use of the profit margin scheme \| 2,500 \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 370 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 346 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| No tax invoice, credit note or alternative document on time; electronic invoice rules not met \| 2,500 for each detected case \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 371 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 347 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Designated Zone goods conditions not met \| Higher of 50,000 or 50% of the tax \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 372 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 348 | Penalties from 14 April 2026: \| Failure to account for import VAT \| 50% of the unpaid or undeclared tax \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 373 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / base / 350 | Record keeping: ## Record keeping ([VAT Law, Article 78](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf); [Executive Regulation, Article 71](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf); [Cabinet Decision No. 74 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2074%20of%202023%20on%20Executive%20Regulation%20of%20Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%2028%20of%202022%20on%20Tax%20Procedures%20-%20For%20Publishing.pdf)) | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 375 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | uae-vat / base / 352 | Record keeping: - Keep: records of all supplies and imports; tax invoices and credit notes issued and received (or alternative documents); records of goods and services used for non-business purposes and of purchases where input tax was not deducted; records of exports; records of adjustments and corrections; and a tax record of due tax, reverse-charge tax, recoverable tax and corrections. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 377 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 3 | frontmatter: description: Use this skill whenever asked to prepare, review, or classify transactions for a UAE VAT return (VAT201 form) for any client. Trigger on phrases like "prepare VAT return", "do the VAT", "fill in VAT201", "create the return", "UAE VAT filing", "FTA return", or any request involving UAE VAT filing. Also trigger when classifying transactions for VAT purposes from bank statements, invoices, or other source data. This skill covers the UAE only and only standard VAT-registered persons filing VAT201. VAT groups, profit margin schemes, partial exemption with non-trivial exempt supplies, and Designated Zone goods movement classifications are all in the refusal catalogue. MUST be loaded alongside vat-workflow-base v0.1 or later (for workflow architecture). ALWAYS read this skill before touching any UAE VAT work. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 3 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 15 | UAE VAT: 2026 registration, rates, VAT201 return, reverse charge, input tax and e-invoicing: # UAE VAT: 2026 registration, rates, VAT201 return, reverse charge, input tax and e-invoicing | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 16 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 17 | UAE VAT: 2026 registration, rates, VAT201 return, reverse charge, input tax and e-invoicing: Tax year 2026. UAE VAT is filed by tax period (usually a quarter), not by annual return, so "2026" here means tax periods that fall in calendar year 2026. The rules below are the law as published on tax.gov.ae and mof.gov.ae on 25 September 2026. Two sets of changes took effect this year (1 January 2026 and 14 April 2026) and one more takes effect on 1 October 2026; each is dated where it applies. A short section covers returns for periods that ended before those dates. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 18 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 19 | Scope and who this is for: ## Scope and who this is for | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 20 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 21 | Scope and who this is for: Sources for this section: [VAT Law, consolidated to Decree-Law No. 16 of 2025](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf); [Executive Regulation, consolidated 09-2026](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 22 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 23 | Scope and who this is for: This skill covers federal Value Added Tax under Federal Decree-Law No. 8 of 2017 ("VAT Law") and its Executive Regulation (Cabinet Decision No. 52 of 2017), for: | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 24 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 25 | Scope and who this is for: - businesses deciding whether they must or may register; | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 26 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 26 | Scope and who this is for: - a single VAT-registered business (not a Tax Group) preparing or reviewing its VAT201 return; | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 27 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 27 | Scope and who this is for: - classifying sales and purchases: standard rate, zero rate, exempt, not a supply, reverse charge, blocked input tax; | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 28 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 28 | Scope and who this is for: - invoicing, record keeping, penalties and the e-invoicing timeline. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 29 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 30 | Scope and who this is for: It does not compute partial-exemption apportionment, Tax Group returns, Designated Zone goods movements, capital asset scheme adjustments, the oil and gas reverse charge, or tourist refund scheme entries. Those are referred out (see "When to refuse or refer"). It does not cover Corporate Tax or Excise Tax. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 31 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 32 | Scope and who this is for: The standard rate is 5% ([VAT Law, Article 3](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)). All amounts on the return are in UAE dirhams (AED). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 33 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 34 | Ask the client first: ## Ask the client first | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 35 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 36 | Ask the client first: - **Is the business registered, and from what date?** Get the TRN, the effective date of registration and the tax period on the registration certificate. The period the FTA assigned governs, not a rule of thumb. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 37 | Ask the client first: - **Is it a single registrant or a member of a Tax Group?** Tax Groups are referred out. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 38 | Ask the client first: - **Where is it established?** Which Emirate (for the Box 1 split), and is it in a Designated Zone, a free zone that is not designated, or the mainland? | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 37 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 39 | Ask the client first: - **What does it sell, and to whom?** Local customers, customers abroad, other GCC countries, consumers or businesses. Any residential property, bare land, financial services, education, healthcare or local passenger transport? | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 38 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 40 | Ask the client first: - **Does it make any exempt supplies?** If more than trivial, input tax must be apportioned: refer. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 38 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 41 | Ask the client first: - **What did it buy from abroad?** Services or software billed by a supplier with no UAE TRN (reverse charge), and goods imported through UAE customs (Box 6). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 39 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 42 | Ask the client first: - **Which costs are entertainment, cars, or staff benefits?** These are the main blocked items. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 39 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 43 | Ask the client first: - **Has it paid its suppliers?** Input tax on an unpaid bill is claimable only if the business intends to pay within six months of the agreed payment date ([Executive Regulation, Article 54(2)](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 40 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 44 | Ask the client first: - **Any errors in earlier returns?** The size of the tax difference decides whether a voluntary disclosure is needed. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 41 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 45 | Ask the client first: - **Excess input tax carried forward?** And from which tax period it arose (the five-year limit applies from 1 January 2026). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 41 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 46 | Ask the client first: - **Revenue in the last accounting period?** It decides the e-invoicing deadline (the AED 50,000,000 test in [Ministerial Decision No. 244 of 2025](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/Ministerial-Decision-No.-244-of-2025-on-the-Implementation-of-the-Electronic-Invoicing-System.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 42 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 48 | The method, step by step: ## The method, step by step | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 44 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 50 | The method, step by step: 1. **Confirm registration.** If not registered, test the mandatory threshold (supplies in the last 12 months, or expected in the next 30 days) and the voluntary threshold ([Executive Regulation, Articles 7 and 8](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). A late registrant owes VAT on supplies made from the date it should have been registered. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 46 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 51 | The method, step by step: 2. **Fix the tax period and deadline.** Take the period from the certificate. The return and the payment must both reach the FTA by the 28th day after the period ends. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 47 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. No claim that a text match itself verifies the law. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 52 | The method, step by step: 3. **Classify each sale.** Standard 5%, zero-rated, exempt, or not a supply ([VAT Law, Articles 3, 45 and 46](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)). Check the zero-rating conditions. Services to an overseas customer are zero-rated only if all of these hold: the customer has no place of residence in a GCC implementing state; the customer is outside the UAE when the services are performed; the services are not directly connected with real estate in the UAE, or with moveable goods in the UAE at the time the services are performed; and the services are not treated as performed in the UAE under the special place-of-supply rules in VAT Law Articles 30(3)-(8) and 31. Services actually performed outside the implementing states are also zero-rated ([Executive Regulation, Article 31(1)](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 48 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 53 | The method, step by step: 4. **Split standard-rated sales by Emirate** for Box 1a-1g. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 49 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 54 | The method, step by step: 5. **Pick up reverse-charge purchases.** Services and goods from suppliers with no UAE place of residence who are not charging UAE VAT go in Box 3 (output VAT) and, if recoverable, Box 10. Imports through UAE customs appear in Box 6; correct them in Box 7. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 50 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 55 | The method, step by step: 6. **Classify each purchase.** Keep only input tax on costs used for taxable supplies, backed by a tax invoice (or import documents), and paid or intended to be paid within six months of the agreed payment date. Strip out blocked items: entertainment of non-employees, motor vehicles available for personal use, and free personal benefits to staff. Conservative defaults where the facts are missing: an item whose blocked status is unknown is treated as blocked; a purchase whose VAT status is unknown is not recovered; if the Emirate of a sale is unknown, ask before filing. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 51 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 56 | The method, step by step: 7. **Deal with credit notes and past errors.** Credit notes reduce the relevant box. Where a past return understated the payable tax: an error of AED 10,000 or less in tax is corrected in the return; above that, file a voluntary disclosure within 20 business days. An error in the business's favour (tax overstated) may be corrected by voluntary disclosure but need not be ([Cabinet Decision No. 74 of 2023](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/Cabinet%20Decision%20No.%2074%20of%202023%20on%20Executive%20Regulation%20of%20Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%2028%20of%202022%20on%20Tax%20Procedures%20-%20For%20Publishing.pdf)). | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 52 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 57 | The method, step by step: 8. **Compute the return.** Box 12 (due tax) less Box 13 (recoverable tax) gives Box 14. If Box 14 is negative, choose a refund or carry the excess forward, noting the five-year limit. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 53 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 58 | The method, step by step: 9. **File and pay** through the FTA's online portal by the 28th day. Keep the records for at least five years. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 54 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 60 | Rates, thresholds and periods for 2026: ## Rates, thresholds and periods for 2026 | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 56 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 67 | Registration: \| When it bites \| A person with a place of residence in the UAE or another GCC implementing state must register where the supplies counted under Article 19 in the previous 12 months **exceeded** the threshold, or are expected to exceed it in the next 30 days \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 63 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 68 | Registration: \| Application deadline \| Within 30 days of becoming required to register \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 64 | Exact text retained. |

Look-forward test: the date there were reasonable grounds to expect the threshold to be exceeded. Either way, whether or not the person applies \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 65 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 70 | Registration: \| Non-resident \| Must register if making supplies in the UAE where no other person is obliged to pay the VAT; registered from the date it started making supplies; no threshold \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 66 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 72 | Registration: \| Voluntary test \| Supplies **or taxable expenses** in the previous 12 months exceeded the voluntary threshold (shown at the end of any month), or are expected to in the next 30 days. "Taxable expenses" means standard-rated expenses incurred in the UAE by a person with a place of residence in the UAE. The person must also prove it carries on a business in the UAE and intends to make taxable (or equivalent) supplies. Registration takes effect from the first day of the month after the application, or an earlier agreed date \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 68 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 73 | Registration: \| Late registration \| VAT is due on all taxable supplies and imports made before registering \| | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 69 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 125 | The VAT201 return, box by box: - Supplier bad debt relief ([VAT Law, Article 64(2)](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2025/Federal%20Decree-Law%20No.%208%20of%202017%20and%20amendments%20-%20publishing%2028%2011%202025.pdf)): the buyer must reduce its recoverable input tax only when all of these hold: the supplier has written off the debt, reduced its output tax and notified the buyer; the buyer deducted the input tax; and the consideration has been unpaid, in full or in part, for over 6 months. | uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 157 | Input tax recovery and blocked items: **Change from 1 October 2026** ([Cabinet Decision No. 149 of 2026](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Cabinet-Decision-No.-149-of-2026-Amending-Certain-Provisions-of-The-Executive-Regulation-of-VAT-EN.pdf)): the labour-law exception excludes staff accommodation unless the Ministry of Human Resources and Emiratisation makes it mandatory; the contract-or-policy exception applies "in accordance with the cases and conditions specified by the Authority" (until 30 September 2026 the goods or services must be provided "in order that they may perform their role" and this must be shown to be normal business practice, [Executive Regulation as at October 2025](https://mof.gov.ae/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/Cabinet-Decision-No.-52-of-2017-of-the-Executive-Regulation-of-the-Federal-Decree-Law-No.-8-of-2017-on-Value-Added-Tax-and-its-amendments.pdf)). | uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 198 | Profit margin scheme: - Available for second-hand goods, antiques (over 50 years old) and collectors' items that have already borne VAT, bought from a non-registrant or from a seller who used the margin scheme; also for goods on which input tax was blocked under Executive Regulation Article 53. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 196 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 199 | Profit margin scheme: - VAT is charged on the margin (selling price less purchase price), which is treated as VAT-inclusive. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 197 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 200 | Profit margin scheme: - Not available if the invoice for the sale shows an amount of VAT. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 198 | Explicit source or condition correction, mapped to its actual repaired paragraph. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 201 | Profit margin scheme: - The seller keeps a stock book and purchase invoices (self-issued where bought from a non-registrant) and issues a tax invoice stating that VAT was charged on the margin, without showing the VAT amount. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 199 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 202 | Profit margin scheme: - The purchase price includes costs or fees incurred to buy the goods. From 1 October 2026 it includes only those costs or fees whose input tax is not recoverable, which narrows the purchase price and so can increase the margin. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 200 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 203 | Profit margin scheme: - The business must notify the FTA that it uses the scheme; failing to is a penalty of AED 2,500. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 201 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 254 | Worked cases: **Case 1: mandatory registration.** A Dubai mainland trading LLC, not registered, has taxable supplies of AED 390,000 in the 12 months to 30 June 2026. That exceeds AED 375,000, so it must apply within 30 days of becoming required to register. The FTA registers it from the first day of the month after the month in which it became required to register, whether or not it applies, and it owes VAT on its taxable supplies from that date. A late application is a penalty of AED 10,000. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 253 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 256 | Worked cases: **Case 2: voluntary registration.** A start-up has no sales yet but taxable expenses of AED 200,000 in the last 12 months. That exceeds AED 187,500, so it may register voluntarily and recover input tax. It is not required to register. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 255 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 258 | Worked cases: **Case 3: reverse charge on software.** A registered Dubai consultancy pays a US software company AED 3,672 for a subscription. The supplier has no UAE TRN and charges no VAT. Box 3: amount AED 3,672, VAT 3,672 x 5% = AED 183.60. Box 10: the same amounts, because the consultancy makes only taxable supplies. Net effect nil. For a supply received after 1 January 2026 no self-invoice is issued; the supplier's invoice is kept. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 257 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 307 | Worked cases: **Case 5: gift limit.** In the 12 months to 31 August 2026 a business gives commercial gifts of goods on which it recovered input tax. One customer receives goods costing AED 400: no deemed supply (not above AED 500). Another receives goods costing AED 700: that is a deemed supply reported in Box 1, subject to the AED 2,000 relief on total output tax on deemed supplies in the 12 months. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 323 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 309 | Worked cases: **Case 6: correcting an error.** In September 2026 a business finds it under-declared output tax of AED 12,000 in the quarter to 31 March 2026. That is above AED 10,000, so it files a voluntary disclosure within 20 business days of finding the error. If the difference had been AED 8,000 it would correct it in the next return instead. | REPAIRED / RELOCATED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 325 | Source/condition/shape repair; compare the complete old and new line and the operative ledger. | uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 311 | When to refuse or refer: ## When to refuse or refer | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 327 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 313 | When to refuse or refer: - **Tax Group members:** consolidation and intra-group supplies need a registered tax agent. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 329 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 314 | When to refuse or refer: - **Partial exemption:** any business with more than incidental exempt supplies (residential property, margin-based financial services) needs apportionment of mixed input tax, and a new method applies from the first tax year beginning after 1 October 2027. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 330 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 315 | When to refuse or refer: - **Designated Zone goods movements:** goods entering, leaving or moving between Designated Zones. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 331 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 316 | When to refuse or refer: - **Capital asset scheme:** assets costing AED 5,000,000 or more (excluding VAT), on which VAT is payable, with a useful life of 10 years or more for buildings, 5 years or more otherwise ([Executive Regulation, Article 57](https://tax.gov.ae/Datafolder/Files/Legislation/2026/Law-No-8-of-2017-and-its-amendments--09-2026.pdf)). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 332 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 317 | When to refuse or refer: - **Oil, gas and hydrocarbons** between registrants (domestic reverse charge) and other special reverse charges. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 333 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 318 | When to refuse or refer: - **Tourist Refund Scheme** operators or retailers. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 334 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 319 | When to refuse or refer: - **Complex profit margin scheme** cases (mixed stock, second-hand vehicles, partial exports). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 335 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 320 | When to refuse or refer: - **Tax evasion chain notices** from the FTA, audits, objections and reconsideration requests. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 336 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 321 | When to refuse or refer: - **Penalties for violations that began before 14 April 2026:** the decision text we found does not say whether the new table applies to them; check with the FTA. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 337 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 322 | When to refuse or refer: - **Cash-payment input tax block** from 1 October 2026, until the Minister's decision setting the value is found. | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 338 | Exact text retained. |
| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 323 | When to refuse or refer: - **GCC implementing-state supplies** (goods or services to or from other GCC countries). | RETAINED: skills/uae/uae-vat.md line 339 | Exact text retained. |

| uae-vat / candidate-df33d21 / 330 | Returns and payment: - The return and the payment must reach the FTA no later than the 28th day after the end of the tax period, or such other date as the FTA directs (for a quarter ending 30 September 2026, by 28 October 2026).
